============ SETTINGS ============ */ $('settingsFab').onclick = ()=> $('settingsPanel').classList.add('active'); $('closeSettings').onclick = ()=> $('settingsPanel').classList.remove('active'); /* ============ QUESTION SOURCING ============ */ async function fetchGeminiQuestions(levelNum){ try{ const res = await fetch('/.netlify/functions/generate-questions', { method: 'POST', headers: {'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body: JSON.stringify({ level: levelNum, count: QUESTIONS_PER_LEVEL }) }); if(!res.ok) return null; const parsed = await res.json(); if(Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length) return parsed; return null; }catch(e){ console.warn('Gemini function call failed, falling back to local bank', e); return null; } } async function loadLevelQuestions(levelIdx){ const gemini = await fetchGeminiQuestions(levelIdx+1); if(gemini) return gemini; return FALLBACK_BANK[levelIdx % FALLBACK_BANK.length]; } /* ============ TIMER ============ */ function startTimer(){ clearInterval(state.timerHandle); state.timeLeft = TIMER_SECONDS; const circle = $('timerCircle'); const wrap = $('timerWrap'); wrap.classList.remove('urgent'); circle.style.strokeDashoffset = 0; $('timerNum').textContent = state.timeLeft; state.timerHandle = setInterval(()=>{ state.timeLeft -= 1; const pct = state.timeLeft / TIMER_SECONDS; circle.style.strokeDashoffset = TIMER_CIRCUMFERENCE * (1-pct); $('timerNum').textContent = Math.max(state.timeLeft,0); if(state.timeLeft <= 2) wrap.classList.add('urgent'); if(state.timeLeft <= 0){ clearInterval(state.timerHandle); handleAnswer(-1); // time's up } },1000); } /* ============ QUIZ FLOW ============ */ async function beginLevel(){ $('levelLabel').textContent = `লেভেল ${state.level+1}`; $('levelPreview').textContent = `লেভেল ${state.level+1}`; $('questionText').textContent = 'প্রশ্ন তৈরি হচ্ছে…'; showScreen('quiz'); state.currentLevelQuestions = await loadLevelQuestions(state.level); state.qIndex = 0; renderQuestion(); } function renderQuestion(){ state.locked = false; const item = state.currentLevelQuestions[state.qIndex]; $('qCounter').textContent = `প্রশ্ন ${state.qIndex+1} / ${QUESTIONS_PER_LEVEL}`; $('progressFill').style.width = `${(state.qIndex/QUESTIONS_PER_LEVEL)*100}%`; $('questionText').textContent = item.q; const wrap = $('optionsWrap'); wrap.innerHTML = ''; item.opts.forEach((opt, i)=>{ const btn = document.createElement('button'); btn.className = 'opt-btn'; btn.textContent = opt; btn.onclick = ()=> handleAnswer(i); wrap.appendChild(btn); }); startTimer(); } function handleAnswer(selectedIdx){ if(state.locked) return; state.locked = true; clearInterval(state.timerHandle); const item = state.currentLevelQuestions[state.qIndex]; const buttons = [...$('optionsWrap').children]; buttons.forEach((b,i)=>{ b.disabled = true; if(i === item.ans) b.classList.add('correct'); else if(i === selectedIdx) b.classList.add('wrong'); }); if(selectedIdx === item.ans) state.score += 1; state.totalAnswered += 1; setTimeout(()=>{ state.qIndex += 1; if(state.qIndex >= QUESTIONS_PER_LEVEL){ $('progressFill').style.width = '100%'; $('unlockModal').classList.add('active'); } else { renderQuestion(); } }, 900); } /* ============ AD UNLOCK (simulated) ============ */ $('watchAdBtn').onclick = ()=>{ $('watchAdBtn').disabled = true; $('watchAdBtn').textContent = 'বিজ্ঞাপন দেখা হচ্ছে…'; $('adProgress').style.display = 'block'; let p = 0; const fill = $('adProgressFill'); const iv = setInterval(()=>{ p += 4; fill.style.width = p+'%'; if(p>=100){ clearInterval(iv); unlockNextLevel(); } }, 90); // ~2.2s simulated ad }; $('skipAdBtn').onclick = ()=>{ $('unlockModal').classList.remove('active'); showResult(); }; // Call this from your native AdMob rewarded-ad "onUserEarnedReward" callback // e.g. via a WebView JS bridge: window.unlockNextLevel() function unlockNextLevel(){ $('unlockModal').classList.remove('active'); $('watchAdBtn').disabled = false; $('watchAdBtn').textContent = 'বিজ্ঞাপন দেখে আনলক করুন'; $('adProgress').style.display = 'none'; $('adProgressFill').style.width = '0%'; state.level += 1; beginLevel(); } window.unlockNextLevel = unlockNextLevel; /* ============ RESULT ============ */ function showResult(){ $('finalScore').textContent = `${state.score}/${state.totalAnswered}`; showScreen('result'); } /* ============ START / RESTART ============ */ $('startBtn').onclick = ()=>{ state.level = 0; state.score = 0; state.totalAnswered = 0; beginLevel(); }; $('restartBtn').onclick = ()=> showScreen('landing');
